# 子 Agent · 你负责 ShopStack 项目的 **Vue3 Web 用户端（`apps/web-vue/`）** 从 30 页扩到 **80 页**。

> 第 1 / 2 卷

## 👤 用户 · 2026-10-20 14:27:40

你负责 ShopStack 项目的 **Vue3 Web 用户端（`apps/web-vue/`）** 从 30 页扩到 **80 页**。

## 项目背景

ShopStack 是一个中大型电商项目（Go 微服务后端 + Flutter 移动端 + 微信小程序 + Vue3 Web 用户端 + React 后台管理端）。
本轮任务是把**每一端**各自扩到 80 页。你负责 Vue3 Web 用户端这一端。

工作目录：`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack`

## 必读文档（先读这两份）

1. **`docs/page-expansion-plan.md`** —— 页面清单的**唯一来源**。里面有 Vue3 端需要新增的 50 个页面的完整清单
   （编号、页面名、View 文件名、数据来源接口）。严格按清单实现，不要自行增删页面。
2. **`docs/frontend-worker-brief.md`** —— 通用规则：目录所有权、Mock 兜底、金额格式、接口调用规范、
   端口提示、设计令牌、验收命令。这份文件里的所有规则对你同样生效。

## 你的唯一所有权范围

- 你**只能**修改 `apps/web-vue/**` 下的文件
- **严禁**修改 `apps/flutter/`、`apps/miniapp/`、`apps/admin-react/`、`backend/`、`proto/`、`gen/`、`docs/`、
  `scripts/`、`deploy/`、`design-tokens/`。其他 worker 正在并行修改这些目录，你改会冲突。
- 设计令牌在 `design-tokens/generated/`（只读）。新增页面必须用已有令牌变量，不要硬编码颜色和间距。

## 现有代码结构速览（先摸清再动手）

```
apps/web-vue/src/
├── api/          # auth.ts cart.ts client.ts logistics.ts marketing.ts notification.ts
│                 # order.ts payment.ts product.ts user.ts
├── components/   # ProductCard.vue PriceText.vue Pagination.vue EmptyState.vue LoadingState.vue
│                 # Skeleton.vue DemoBanner.vue StarRating.vue AddressPicker.vue 等 17 个
├── views/        # 30 个 *View.vue（你要扩到 80 个）
├── router/index.ts   # 30 条路由，与 views 严格 1:1
├── stores/       # Pinia
├── utils/        # auth.ts enums.ts format.ts image.ts
├── types/        # TS 类型定义
└── mock/         # Mock 数据
```

**先读** `src/views/` 下 3-4 个已有页面（如 `HomeView.vue`、`CategoryView.vue`、`OrderListView.vue`、
`CouponsView.vue`）摸清既有风格：**Vue 3 `<script setup lang="ts">` + Composition API + TypeScript**、
scoped 样式、复用 `src/components/` 的卡片/分页/空状态组件、复用 `src/api/` 的请求函数。
**不要另起炉灶写一套风格。**

## 关键实现要求

### 1. 路由与页面严格 1:1

`src/views/` 每个 `*View.vue` 必须有且仅有一条对应路由。扩完后：
- views 数 == 80
- 路由数 == 80（`/:pathMatch(.*)*` 的 404 路由不计入 view 数，但 `NotFoundView.vue` 占一个 view）

新增路由时：
- **组件级懒加载**：`() => import('@/views/XxxView.vue')`（现有代码是这么做的，保持一致）
- 保持现有的路由元信息风格（meta 里的 title 等，照抄现有写法）

### 2. API 层

新页面需要的接口，在 `src/api/` 对应域文件里加请求函数（`product.ts` / `user.ts` / `order.ts` /
`marketing.ts` / `payment.ts` / `logistics.ts` / `notification.ts`），复用 `src/api/client.ts` 的
`request` 实例。

分页参数用**点号嵌套**形式（`client.ts` 已有 `serializeParams` 处理，参考现有写法）。
响应信封 `{ code, message, data, trace_id }`，`code === 0` 成功。

新增接口的后端路径清单见 `docs/page-expansion-plan.md` 里每个页面的「数据来源」列。

### 3. Mock 兜底（必须）

后端部分接口可能还没就绪。沿用现有 Mock 机制：`src/api/client.ts` 里已有 Mock 分支，
`src/mock/` 里提供数据。新增接口的 Mock 数据要**结构与真实接口完全一致**（snake_case 字段名），
并保证「演示数据模式」提示条能正常显示（复用 `DemoBanner.vue`）。

### 4. 金额

后端返回**分**（int64）。展示必须用 `src/utils/format.ts` 的 `formatYuan(cents)` 或
`PriceText.vue` 组件，禁止直接展示数字。

### 5. 禁止事项

- 禁止 TODO / FIXME / 「敬请期待」/「开发中」等占位文案
- 禁止空 `onClick` / 死按钮
- 禁止复制粘贴同一页面改标题充数 —— 参数化页面必须**共用组件**，通过路由参数/查询参数区分
- 禁止「暂无数据」之外的无意义空状态

### 6. 端口提示（重要，别误判）

本机 **8080 端口被无关进程占用**（进程名 `books-api`，非本项目）。
本地连后端用 **8090**：`VITE_GATEWAY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090`。
后端健康检查：`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8090/healthz` 返回 `{"status":"ok"}` 即正常。
如果看到「登录被拦截 / 401」，先确认后端是否在跑，**不要去改后端代码**。

## 验收（必须自检并在最终回复中给出实际输出）

```bash
cd /<WORKSPACE>/shopstack/apps/web-vue
npm run build
```

另外自查并报告：
- views 数量（用 `ls src/views/*View.vue | wc -l`）
- 路由数量（用 `grep -c "path:" src/router/index.ts`）
- 两者是否都是 80
- `npm run build` 的完整输出（有无 chunk 体积告警）

如果 `npm run build` 报 chunk 体积告警（>500KB），沿用现有的 `manualChunks` 分包配置调整，
让告警清零。

## 交付要求

最终回复里给出：
1. 新增的 50 个页面逐个列出（View 文件名 + 中文页面名）
2. 新建/修改了哪些文件（路径列表）
3. 上面几条自检命令的**实际输出**（粘贴真实结果，不要写"应该没问题"）
4. views 数 / 路由数统计结果
5. 诚实说明没做完或有疑问的部分

**禁止虚报**。没做完就说没做完。

<details><summary>🛠 read</summary>

````
1→# 阶段 3 前端 worker 通用须知
     2→
     3→以下 4 个 brief 共同引用本文件内容。派发时请把本文件路径告诉 worker：
     4→`docs/page-expansion-plan.md`（页面清单，唯一来源）
     5→
     6→## 共同背景
     7→
     8→ShopStack 是中大型电商项目：Go 微服务后端（9 服务）+ Flutter 移动端 + 微信小程序 +
     9→Vue3 Web 用户端 + React 后台管理端。本轮任务是把**每一端**从当前页面数扩到 **80 页**。
    10→
    11→工作目录：`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack`
    12→
    13→## 页面清单来源
    14→
    15→**必须先读 `docs/page-expansion-plan.md`**，里面有每一端需要新增的页面的完整清单
    16→（编号、页面名、文件路径、数据来源接口）。严格按清单实现，不要自行增删页面。
    17→
    18→## 目录所有权（互不重叠，禁止越界）
    19→
    20→| Worker | 可写目录 | 禁止触碰 |
    21→|---|---|---|
    22→| Flutter | `apps/flutter/**` | 其他三个前端目录、backend/、proto/、gen/、docs/、scripts/、deploy/ |
    23→| 小程序 | `apps/miniapp/**` | 同上 |
    24→| Vue3 | `apps/web-vue/**` | 同上 |
    25→| React | `apps/admin-react/**` | 同上 |
    26→
    27→各 worker 之间**不得互相修改对方的目录**。后端目录 `backend/` 已被阶段 2 的 worker 修改，
    28→前端 worker 一律不得改动。
    29→
    30→## 硬性规则（违反即返工）
    31→
    32→### 1. 禁止占位
    33→
    34→- 禁止 TODO / FIXME / 「敬请期待」/「开发中」/「功能上线中」等占位文案
    35→- 禁止空 `onClick` / `@click` 不带处理函数 / 点了没反应的死按钮
    36→- 禁止「暂无数据」之外的无意义空状态（空状态必须有引导操作）
    37→- 禁止复制粘贴同一个页面改标题充数（参数化页面必须共用组件，只有路径/标题/筛选参数不同）
    38→
    39→### 2. Mock 兜底（必须）
    40→
    41→后端可能还没就绪或部分接口未实现。前端必须有 Mock 兜底：
    42→- 请求失败 / `code != 0` / 超时 → 回落到本地 Mock 数据
    43→- 页面顶部显示「演示数据模式」提示（各端已有 `DemoBanner` / `DemoModeAlert` 等组件，沿用）
    44→- Mock 数据的 JSON 结构必须与真实接口**完全一致**（snake_case 字段名）
    45→
    46→各端 Mock 机制位置：
    47→- Flutter：`lib/mock/`，API 层 `lib/api/*.dart`
    48→- 小程序：`utils/mock.js`（内存态，写操作会真实改变状态），`utils/request.js` 里已有兜底逻辑
    49→- Vue3：`src/api/client.ts` 里有 Mock 分支，各 `src/api/*.ts` 提供数据
    50→- React：`src/mock/`（`data.ts` + `index.ts`），`src/store/mock` 控制开关
    51→
    52→### 3. 金额
    53→
    54→后端返回**分**（int64）。展示时必须用各端已有的 `formatYuan(cents)`（Vue3/小程序）
    55→或 `PriceText` 组件（Vue3）/ 等价工具（Flutter `lib/utils/`、React `src/utils/`）。
    56→禁止直接展示 `12345` 这种数字。
    57→
    58→### 4. 接口调用规范
    59→
    60→- 分页 query 用**点号嵌套**：`?page.page=1&page.page_size=20`
    61→- 响应信封：`{ code, message, data, trace_id }`，`code === 0` 为成功
    62→- JSON 字段名 **snake_case**（与 proto 一致）
    63→- 64 位整数是 JSON **数字**
    64→
    65→### 5. 端口提示（重要，别误判）
    66→
    67→本机 **8080 端口被无关进程占用**（进程名 `books-api`，不是本项目）。
    68→本地连后端请用 **8090**：
    69→```bash
    70→VITE_GATEWAY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090     # Vue3
    71→VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090    # React
    72→```
    73→如果你连 8090 看到「登录被拦截 / 401」，先确认后端是否在跑：
    74→```bash
    75→curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8090/healthz
    76→```
    77→返回 `{"status":"ok"}` 说明后端正常。**不要因此去改后端代码**。
    78→
    79→### 6. 设计令牌
    80→
    81→设计令牌在 `design-tokens/generated/`，各端已有对应的样式文件（CSS 变量 / TS 常量 /
    82→Dart 常量 / WXSS 变量）。新增页面必须用已有令牌，**不要硬编码颜色和间距**。
    83→
    84→### 7. 视觉与响应式
    85→
    86→- 移动端（Flutter / 小程序）：375px 宽下无横向滚动、无元素重叠
    87→- Web（Vue3 / React）：1440px 宽下布局正常；后台表格在窄屏下可横向滚动
    88→- 沿用各端现有的卡片、列表、表格、空状态、加载态组件
    89→
    90→### 8. 交付自检（必须在最终回复里给出实际输出）
    91→
    92→每个 worker 交付时必须报告：
    93→1. 新增了哪些页面（逐个列出）
    94→2. 新建/修改了哪些文件（路径列表）
    95→3. 自检命令的**实际输出**（不是"应该没问题"）
    96→4. 页面数统计结果（用什么命令数的）
    97→5. 诚实说明没做完或有疑问的部分
    98→
    99→**禁止虚报**。如果某个页面没做完或某个命令失败了，如实说。
   100→
   101→## 验收命令速查
   102→
   103→```bash
   104→# Flutter
   105→cd apps/flutter && flutter analyze          # 必须 0 error 0 warning
   106→cd apps/flutter && flutter test             # 必须全绿
   107→ls lib/pages/*/*_page.dart | wc -l          # == 80
   108→
   109→# 小程序
   110→node scripts/validate-miniapp.mjs           # 若项目内无此脚本则自写校验
   111→# 校验 app.json pages[] + subPackages[].pages[] 合计 == 80
   112→# 每页 .js/.json/.wxml/.wxss 四件套齐全且 JSON 可解析
   113→# 所有 bindtap/catchtap 绑定的事件都有对应处理函数
   114→
   115→# Vue3
   116→cd apps/web-vue && npm run build            # 必须成功
   117→# views 数 == routes 数 == 80
   118→
   119→# React
   120→cd apps/admin-react && npm run build        # 必须成功
   121→# pages 数（含 system/）== routes 数 == 80
   122→```
````

1→import axios, {
     2→  type AxiosError,
     3→  type AxiosInstance,
     4→  type AxiosRequestConfig,
     5→  type InternalAxiosRequestConfig,
     6→} from 'axios'
     7→
     8→import { useAppStore } from '@/stores/app'
     9→import {
    10→  clearAuthStorage,
    11→  getAccessToken,
    12→  getRefreshToken,
    13→  setAccessToken,
    14→  setRefreshToken,
    15→} from '@/utils/auth'
    16→
    17→/** 网关地址；默认走 Vite 代理（同源 /api），生产环境可用 VITE_API_BASE_URL 指定 */
    18→export const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? ''
    19→
    20→/** 统一响应信封 */
    21→interface ApiEnvelope<T> {
    22→  code: number
    23→  message: string
    24→  data: T
    25→  trace_id: string
    26→}
    27→
    28→/** 业务异常，携带后端返回的 code 与 trace_id */
    29→export class ApiError extends Error {
    30→  /** 后端业务码，部分网关返回字符串码 */
    31→  code: number | string
    32→  status: number
    33→  traceId: string
    34→
    35→  constructor(message: string, code: number | string = -1, status = 0, traceId = '') {
    36→    super(message)
    37→    this.name = 'ApiError'
    38→    this.code = code
    39→    this.status = status
    40→    this.traceId = traceId
    41→  }
    42→}
    43→
    44→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→ * 响应键名归一化
    46→ * 后端可能返回 proto 字段名（snake_case）或 JSON 名（camelCase），
    47→ * 这里统一转换为 snake_case，与 TS 类型定义保持一致。
    48→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
    49→const MAP_FIELDS = new Set(['specs', 'pay_params'])
    50→
    51→function toSnake(key: string): string {
    52→  return key
    53→    .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2')
    54→    .replace(/([A-Z]+)([A-Z][a-z])/g, '$1_$2')
    55→    .toLowerCase()
    56→}
    57→
    58→function normalizeKeys(value: unknown): unknown {
    59→  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => normalizeKeys(item))
    60→  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    61→    const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    62→    for (const [rawKey, rawValue] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    63→      const key = toSnake(rawKey)
    64→      // map 类型字段（specs / pay_params）的 key 属于业务数据，不做转换
    65→      result[key] = MAP_FIELDS.has(key) ? rawValue : normalizeKeys(rawValue)
    66→    }
    67→    return result
    68→  }
    69→  return value
    70→}
    71→
    72→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73→ * Axios 实例
    74→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
    75→const client: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
    76→  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
    77→  timeout: 12000,
    78→  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    79→})
    80→
    81→// 用于刷新令牌的裸实例，避免触发拦截器递归
    82→const plainClient: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
    83→  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
    84→  timeout: 12000,
    85→  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    86→})
    87→
    88→/** 最近一次 trace_id，便于排查问题 */
    89→let lastTraceId = ''
    90→
    91→export function getLastTraceId(): string {
    92→  return lastTraceId
    93→}
    94→
    95→interface RetryConfig extends InternalAxiosRequestConfig {
    96→  _retried?: boolean
    97→  _skipAuth?: boolean
    98→}
    99→
   100→client.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
   101→  const token = getAccessToken()
   102→  if (token) {
   103→    config.headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`)
   104→  }
   105→  return config
   106→})
   107→
   108→/** 是否已处于演示数据模式（后端不可用，接口回退到 Mock 数据） */
   109→function isDemoMode(): boolean {
   110→  try {
   111→    return useAppStore().demoMode
   112→  } catch {
   113→    return false
   114→  }
   115→}
   116→
   117→client.interceptors.response.use(
   118→  (response) => {
   119→    const body = response.data as ApiEnvelope<unknown> | undefined
   120→    // 统一信封：{ code, message, data, trace_id }
   121→    if (body && typeof body === 'object' && 'code' in body) {
   122→      const envelope = body as ApiEnvelope<unknown>
   123→      lastTraceId = envelope.trace_id ?? ''
   124→      if (envelope.code !== 0) {
   125→        throw new ApiError(
   126→          envelope.message || '请求失败',
   127→          envelope.code,
   128→          response.status,
   129→          lastTraceId,
   130→        )
   131→      }
   132→      response.data = normalizeKeys(envelope.data)
   133→      return response
   134→    }
   135→    // 兼容未包装信封的直返结构
   136→    response.data = normalizeKeys(response.data)
   137→    return response
   138→  },
   139→  async (error: AxiosError) => {
   140→    const config = error.config as RetryConfig | undefined
   141→    const status = error.response?.status ?? 0
   142→
   143→    // 401：静默刷新令牌后重放一次原请求
   144→    // 演示模式下后端会拒绝本地 Mock 令牌，此时既不刷新也不清空登录态，页面继续使用演示数据
   145→    if (status === 401 && config && !config._retried && !config._skipAuth && !isDemoMode()) {
   146→      config._retried = true
   147→      const refreshed = await refreshTokens()
   148→      if (refreshed) {
   149→        return client.request(config)
   150→      }
   151→      // 刷新失败：标记为登录态失效，由上层决定是降级到演示数据还是退出登录
   152→      authFailedErrors.add(error)
   153→    }
   154→    return Promise.reject(error)
   155→  },
   156→)
   157→
   158→/** 刷新令牌失败、登录态已失效的请求 */
   159→const authFailedErrors = new WeakSet<object>()
   160→
   161→function isAuthFailed(error: unknown): boolean {
   162→  return typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && authFailedErrors.has(error)
   163→}
   164→
   165→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→ * 令牌刷新（并发共享同一个 Promise，只重放一次）
   167→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
   168→let refreshPromise: Promise<boolean> | null = null
   169→
   170→async function refreshTokens(): Promise<boolean> {
   171→  if (!refreshPromise) {
   172→    refreshPromise = doRefresh().finally(() => {
   173→      refreshPromise = null
   174→    })
   175→  }
   176→  return refreshPromise
   177→}
   178→
   179→async function doRefresh(): Promise<boolean> {
   180→  const refreshToken = getRefreshToken()
   181→  if (!refreshToken) return false
   182→  try {
   183→    const response = await plainClient.post('/api/v1/auth/refresh', { refresh_token: refreshToken })
   184→    const body = response.data as ApiEnvelope<{ access_token: string; refresh_token?: string }>
   185→    if (body && body.code === 0 && body.data?.access_token) {
   186→      setAccessToken(body.data.access_token)
   187→      if (body.data.refresh_token) setRefreshToken(body.data.refresh_token)
   188→      return true
   189→    }
   190→    return false
   191→  } catch {
   192→    return false
   193→  }
   194→}
   195→
   196→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→ * 请求封装
   198→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
   199→export interface RequestOptions<T> {
   200→  /** 后端不可用时的兜底数据 */
   201→  fallback?: () => T
   202→  /** 静默模式：不弹出全局错误提示，由页面自行展示 */
   203→  silent?: boolean
   204→  /** 跳过 401 自动刷新（登录、注册等接口使用） */
   205→  skipAuth?: boolean
   206→}
   207→
   208→function toApiError(error: unknown): ApiError {
   209→  if (error instanceof ApiError) return error
   210→  if (axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
   211→    const axiosError = error as AxiosError<ApiEnvelope<unknown>>
   212→    const status = axiosError.response?.status ?? 0
   213→    const body = axiosError.response?.data
   214→    let message = axiosError.message || '网络请求失败'
   215→    let code: number | string = -1
   216→    let traceId = ''
   217→    if (body && typeof body === 'object' && 'message' in body) {
   218→      message = body.message || message
   219→      code = body.code ?? -1
   220→      traceId = body.trace_id ?? ''
   221→    } else if (status === 0) {
   222→      message = '无法连接服务器，请检查后端服务是否已启动'
   223→    } else if (status === 404) {
   224→      message = '接口不存在，演示数据模式已启用'
   225→    }
   226→    return new ApiError(message, code, status, traceId)
   227→  }
   228→  if (error instanceof Error) return new ApiError(error.message)
   229→  return new ApiError('未知错误')
   230→}
   231→
   232→/** 判断是否适合降级到 Mock 数据：网络不通、404、5xx、401 */
   233→function canFallback(error: ApiError): boolean {
   234→  if (error.status === 0) return true
   235→  if (error.status === 404) return true
   236→  if (error.status === 401) return true
   237→  return error.status >= 500
   238→}
   239→
   240→function cleanParams(params?: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
   241→  if (!params) return {}
   242→  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
   243→  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
   244→    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') continue
   245→    result[key] = value
   246→  }
   247→  return result
   248→}
   249→
   250→async function request<T>(
   251→  config: AxiosRequestConfig,
   252→  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
   253→): Promise<T> {
   254→  try {
   255→    const response = await client.request<unknown>(config)
   256→    return response.data as T
   257→  } catch (error) {
   258→    const apiError = toApiError(error)
   259→    if (options.fallback && canFallback(apiError)) {
   260→      try {
   261→        useAppStore().enterDemoMode(apiError.message)
   262→      } catch {
   263→        // Pinia 尚未就绪时忽略，页面仍会渲染 Mock 数据
   264→      }
   265→      // 演示数据模式下保留本地登录态，避免演示会话被误判为登录失效
   266→      return options.fallback()
   267→    }
   268→    if (isAuthFailed(error)) {
   269→      clearAuthStorage()
   270→      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ss:unauthorized'))
   271→    }
   272→    if (!options.silent) {
   273→      try {
   274→        useAppStore().error(apiError.message)
   275→      } catch {
   276→        // 忽略提示失败
   277→      }
   278→    }
   279→    throw apiError
   280→  }
   281→}
   282→
   283→/** GET 请求 */
   284→export function httpGet<T>(
   285→  url: string,
   286→  params?: Record<string, unknown>,
   287→  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
   288→): Promise<T> {
   289→  return request<T>({ method: 'GET', url, params: cleanParams(params) }, options)
   290→}
   291→
   292→/** POST 请求 */
   293→export function httpPost<T>(
   294→  url: string,
   295→  data?: unknown,
   296→  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
   297→): Promise<T> {
   298→  return request<T>({ method: 'POST', url, data }, options)
   299→}
   300→
   301→/** PUT 请求 */
   302→export function httpPut<T>(
   303→  url: string,
   304→  data?: unknown,
   305→  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
   306→): Promise<T> {
   307→  return request<T>({ method: 'PUT', url, data }, options)
   308→}
   309→
   310→/** DELETE 请求 */
   311→export function httpDelete<T>(
   312→  url: string,
   313→  params?: Record<string, unknown>,
   314→  options: RequestOptions<T> = {},
   315→): Promise<T> {
   316→  return request<T>({ method: 'DELETE', url, params: cleanParams(params) }, options)
   317→}
```

   136→      name: 'refund',
   137→      component: () => import('@/views/RefundView.vue'),
   138→      meta: { title: '售后申请', requiresAuth: true },
   139→    },
   140→    {
   141→      path: '/pay-result',
   142→      name: 'pay-result',
   143→      component: () => import('@/views/PayResultView.vue'),
   144→      meta: { title: '支付结果' },
   145→    },
   146→    {
   147→      path: '/flash-sale',
   148→      name: 'flash-sale',
   149→      component: () => import('@/views/FlashSaleView.vue'),
   150→      meta: { title: '限时秒杀' },
   151→    },
   152→    {
   153→      path: '/group-buy',
   154→      name: 'group-buy',
   155→      component: () => import('@/views/GroupBuyView.vue'),
   156→      meta: { title: '拼团专区' },
   157→    },
   158→    {
   159→      path: '/member',
   160→      name: 'member',
   161→      component: () => import('@/views/MemberView.vue'),
   162→      meta: { title: '会员中心', requiresAuth: true },
   163→    },
   164→    {
   165→      path: '/history',
   166→      name: 'history',
   167→      component: () => import('@/views/HistoryView.vue'),
   168→      meta: { title: '浏览足迹' },
   169→    },
   170→    {
   171→      path: '/compare',
   172→      name: 'compare',
   173→      component: () => import('@/views/CompareView.vue'),
   174→      meta: { title: '商品对比' },
   175→    },
   176→    {
   177→      path: '/categories',
   178→      name: 'categories',
   179→      component: () => import('@/views/CategoriesView.vue'),
   180→      meta: { title: '全部分类' },
   181→    },
   182→    {
   183→      path: '/order-trace',
   184→      name: 'order-trace',
   185→      component: () => import('@/views/OrderTraceView.vue'),
   186→      meta: { title: '订单轨迹' },
   187→    },
   188→    {
   189→      path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*',
   190→      name: 'not-found',
   191→      component: () => import('@/views/NotFoundView.vue'),
   192→      meta: { title: '页面不存在' },
   193→    },
   194→  ],
   195→})
   196→
   197→router.beforeEach((to) => {
   198→  const title = to.meta.title as string | undefined
   199→  document.title = title ? `${title} · ShopStack 商城` : 'ShopStack 商城'
   200→
   201→  if (to.meta.requiresAuth) {
   202→    const auth = useAuthStore()
   203→    if (!auth.isLoggedIn) {
   204→      return { name: 'login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath } }
   205→    }
   206→  }
   207→  return true
   208→})
   209→
   210→export default router
```

<details><summary>🛠 read</summary>

```
1→<script setup lang="ts">
     2→import { onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue'
     3→import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
     4→
     5→import EmptyState from '@/components/EmptyState.vue'
     6→import Pagination from '@/components/Pagination.vue'
     7→import RefundDialog from '@/components/RefundDialog.vue'
     8→import { applyRefund, cancelOrder, confirmReceipt, getOrder, listOrders } from '@/api/order'
     9→import { useAppStore } from '@/stores/app'
    10→import { useCartStore } from '@/stores/cart'
    11→import type { Order, OrderItem, PageMeta } from '@/types'
    12→import { orderStatusText, orderStatusTone } from '@/utils/enums'
    13→import { formatDateTime, formatYuan } from '@/utils/format'
    14→import { handleImageError } from '@/utils/image'
    15→
    16→const route = useRoute()
    17→const router = useRouter()
    18→const app = useAppStore()
    19→const cart = useCartStore()
    20→
    21→const tabs = [
    22→  { value: 0, label: '全部' },
    23→  { value: 1, label: '待付款' },
    24→  { value: 2, label: '待发货' },
    25→  { value: 3, label: '待收货' },
    26→  { value: 6, label: '退款' },
    27→]
    28→
    29→const activeTab = ref(Number(route.query.status ?? 0))
    30→const orders = ref<Order[]>([])
    31→/** 订单号 → 商品条目，用于展示缩略图 */
    32→const orderItems = ref<Record<number, OrderItem[]>>({})
    33→const meta = ref<PageMeta>({ page: 1, page_size: 10, total: 0, total_pages: 1 })
    34→const loading = ref(false)
    35→const refundVisible = ref(false)
    36→const refundOrder = ref<Order | null>(null)
    37→
    38→const PAGE_SIZE = 10
    39→
    40→async function load(): Promise<void> {
    41→  loading.value = true
    42→  try {
    43→    const result = await listOrders({
    44→      page: meta.value.page,
    45→      page_size: PAGE_SIZE,
    46→      status: activeTab.value || undefined,
    47→    })
    48→    orders.value = result.orders
    49→    meta.value = result.meta
    50→    await loadItems(result.orders)
    51→  } catch {
    52→    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
    53→  } finally {
    54→    loading.value = false
    55→  }
    56→}
    57→
    58→/** 列表接口只返回订单概要，这里补齐商品条目用于缩略图展示 */
    59→async function loadItems(list: Order[]): Promise<void> {
    60→  const entries = await Promise.all(
    61→    list.map(async (order) => {
    62→      try {
    63→        const detail = await getOrder(order.id)
    64→        return [order.id, detail.items] as const
    65→      } catch {
    66→        return [order.id, [] as OrderItem[]] as const
    67→      }
    68→    }),
    69→  )
    70→  orderItems.value = Object.fromEntries(entries)
    71→}
    72→
    73→function changeTab(value: number): void {
    74→  activeTab.value = value
    75→  meta.value = { ...meta.value, page: 1 }
    76→  load()
    77→}
    78→
    79→function changePage(page: number): void {
    80→  meta.value = { ...meta.value, page }
    81→  load()
    82→  window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
    83→}
    84→
    85→async function onCancel(order: Order): Promise<void> {
    86→  try {
    87→    await cancelOrder(order.id, '用户主动取消')
    88→    app.success('订单已取消')
    89→    load()
    90→  } catch {
    91→    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
    92→  }
    93→}
    94→
    95→async function onConfirm(order: Order): Promise<void> {
    96→  try {
    97→    await confirmReceipt(order.id)
    98→    app.success('已确认收货')
    99→    load()
   100→  } catch {
   101→    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
   102→  }
   103→}
   104→
   105→function openRefund(order: Order): void {
   106→  refundOrder.value = order
   107→  refundVisible.value = true
   108→}
   109→
   110→async function submitRefund(payload: { reason: string; description: string }): Promise<void> {
   111→  if (!refundOrder.value) return
   112→  try {
   113→    await applyRefund({
   114→      order_id: refundOrder.value.id,
   115→      amount_cents: refundOrder.value.payable_cents,
   116→      reason: payload.reason,
   117→      description: payload.description,
   118→    })
   119→    app.success('退款申请已提交')
   120→    load()
   121→  } catch {
   122→    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
   123→  }
   124→}
   125→
   126→/** 再来一单：把订单商品重新加入购物车 */
   127→async function reorder(order: Order): Promise<void> {
   128→  const items = orderItems.value[order.id] ?? []
   129→  if (!items.length) {
   130→    app.warning('该订单没有可再次购买的商品')
   131→    return
   132→  }
   133→  try {
   134→    for (const item of items) {
   135→      await cart.addToCart({
   136→        product_id: item.product_id,
   137→        sku_id: item.sku_id,
   138→        quantity: item.quantity,
   139→      })
   140→    }
   141→    app.success('已加入购物车')
   142→    router.push('/cart')
   143→  } catch {
   144→    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
   145→  }
   146→}
   147→
   148→function goPay(order: Order): void {
   149→  router.push({ name: 'payment', params: { orderId: String(order.id) } })
   150→}
   151→
   152→// 个人中心的订单状态入口通过 ?status= 直达对应 Tab
   153→watch(
   154→  () => route.query.status,
   155→  (value) => {
   156→    const next = Number(value ?? 0)
   157→    if (next !== activeTab.value && tabs.some((tab) => tab.value === next)) {
   158→      activeTab.value = next
   159→      meta.value = { ...meta.value, page: 1 }
   160→      load()
   161→    }
   162→  },
   163→)
   164→
   165→onMounted(load)
   166→</script>
   167→
   168→<template>
   169→  <div class="order-list ss-container ss-page">
   170→    <div class="ss-page-head">
   171→      <div>
   172→        <h1 class="ss-page-title">我的订单</h1>
   173→        <p class="ss-page-sub">查看订单状态、物流进度与售后操作</p>
   174→      </div>
   175→      <div class="ss-row">

<details><summary>🛠 read</summary>

```
1→/**
     2→ * 展示层格式化工具
     3→ * 约定：金额一律以「分」为单位的整数存储，展示时才转换为「元」。
     4→ */
     5→
     6→/** 分 → 展示文本，例如 129900 → ¥1,299.00 */
     7→export function formatYuan(cents?: number | null, withSymbol = true): string {
     8→  const value = Number(cents ?? 0) / 100
     9→  const text = value.toLocaleString('zh-CN', {
    10→    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    11→    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    12→  })
    13→  return withSymbol ? `¥${text}` : text
    14→}
    15→
    16→/** 分 → 紧凑文本，例如 129900 → ¥1,299（用于大额汇总） */
    17→export function formatYuanShort(cents?: number | null): string {
    18→  const value = Number(cents ?? 0) / 100
    19→  if (Math.abs(value) >= 10000) {
    20→    return `¥${(value / 10000).toFixed(2)}万`
    21→  }
    22→  return `¥${value.toLocaleString('zh-CN', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}`
    23→}
    24→
    25→/** 「元」文本 → 分，例如 '12.5' → 1250 */
    26→export function yuanToCents(input: string | number | null | undefined): number {
    27→  if (input === null || input === undefined || input === '') return 0
    28→  const num = typeof input === 'number' ? input : Number.parseFloat(input)
    29→  if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return 0
    30→  return Math.round(num * 100)
    31→}
    32→
    33→/** 千分位数字 */
    34→export function formatNumber(value?: number | null): string {
    35→  return Number(value ?? 0).toLocaleString('zh-CN')
    36→}
    37→
    38→/** 销量等大数字紧凑展示：12345 → 1.2万 */
    39→export function formatCount(value?: number | null): string {
    40→  const num = Number(value ?? 0)
    41→  if (num >= 100000000) return `${(num / 100000000).toFixed(1)}亿`
    42→  if (num >= 10000) return `${(num / 10000).toFixed(1)}万`
    43→  return String(num)
    44→}
    45→
    46→function toDate(value?: string | number | null): Date | null {
    47→  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return null
    48→  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    49→    // 秒级时间戳
    50→    return new Date(value * 1000)
    51→  }
    52→  const parsed = new Date(value)
    53→  return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed
    54→}
    55→
    56→function pad(value: number): string {
    57→  return value < 10 ? `0${value}` : String(value)
    58→}
    59→
    60→/** RFC3339 时间 → 2026-10-20 11:49 */
    61→export function formatDateTime(value?: string | number | null): string {
    62→  const date = toDate(value)
    63→  if (!date) return '—'
    64→  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(
    65→    date.getHours(),
    66→  )}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`
    67→}
    68→
    69→/** RFC3339 时间 → 2026-10-20 */
    70→export function formatDate(value?: string | number | null): string {
    71→  const date = toDate(value)
    72→  if (!date) return '—'
    73→  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}`
    74→}
    75→
    76→/** RFC3339 时间 → 11:49:05 */
    77→export function formatTime(value?: string | number | null): string {
    78→  const date = toDate(value)
    79→  if (!date) return '—'
    80→  return `${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}:${pad(date.getSeconds())}`
    81→}
    82→
    83→/** 相对时间：3 分钟前 / 2 天前 */
    84→export function formatFromNow(value?: string | number | null): string {
    85→  const date = toDate(value)
    86→  if (!date) return '—'
    87→  const diff = Date.now() - date.getTime()
    88→  if (diff < 60_000) return '刚刚'
    89→  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)} 分钟前`
    90→  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)} 小时前`
    91→  if (diff < 2_592_000_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000)} 天前`
    92→  return formatDateTime(value)
    93→}
    94→
    95→/** 手机号脱敏 */
    96→export function maskPhone(phone?: string | null): string {
    97→  if (!phone || phone.length < 7) return phone ?? '—'
    98→  return `${phone.slice(0, 3)}****${phone.slice(-4)}`
    99→}
   100→
   101→/** 地址拼接 */
   102→export function formatAddress(address?: {
   103→  province?: string
   104→  city?: string
   105→  district?: string
   106→  detail?: string
   107→} | null): string {
   108→  if (!address) return '—'
   109→  return [address.province, address.city, address.district, address.detail]
   110→    .filter(Boolean)
   111→    .join(' ')
   112→}
   113→
   114→/** 倒计时文本（秒） */
   115→export function formatCountdown(seconds: number): string {
   116→  if (seconds <= 0) return '00:00:00'
   117→  const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600)
   118→  const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)
   119→  const s = seconds % 60
   120→  return `${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}:${pad(s)}`
   121→}
```

Now let me look at the mock layer (data.ts and index.ts) to understand structure, plus style.css for available classes, and components like ProductCard, Pagination, EmptyState, DemoBanner, PriceText.

Let me also check package.json / vite.config for build config and manualChunks.

Given the scale (50 new views), I need to be efficient. Strategy:
1. Create shared components in `src/components/` for parameterized pages (e.g. `ChannelProductView` base, `ReviewListView` base, `OrderStatusView` base). But wait — the plan says parameterized pages must share components, but each View file must exist and have its own route. So I create e.g. `src/components/channel/ChannelProductPanel.vue` and then 10 thin view files using it. That satisfies "禁止复制粘贴同一页面改标题充数 —— 参数化页面必须共用组件".

Actually a cleaner approach: create wrapper view components in `src/views/` that import a shared panel component from `src/components/`.

Let me check the mock module structure first.

    71→  userCoupons: mockUserCoupons.map((item) => ({ ...item })) as UserCoupon[],
    72→  favorites: [
    73→    {
    74→      id: 1,
    75→      product_id: 3,
    76→      title: '智能运动手表 GT4',
    77→      cover: 'https://placehold.co/600x600/F5811F/FFFFFF?text=Watch+GT4',
    78→      price_cents: 89900,
    79→      created_at: '2026-10-11T02:00:00Z',
    80→    },
    81→    {
    82→      id: 2,
    83→      product_id: 9,
    84→      title: '降噪蓝牙耳机 Air',
    85→      cover: 'https://placehold.co/600x600/121B26/FFFFFF?text=Earphone+Air',
    86→      price_cents: 69900,
    87→      created_at: '2026-10-09T02:00:00Z',
    88→    },
    89→  ] as FavoriteProduct[],
    90→  notifications: mockNotifications.map((item) => ({ ...item })) as Notification[],
    91→  payments: mockPayments.map((item) => ({ ...item })) as Payment[],
    92→}
    93→
    94→let cartSeq = 100
    95→let orderSeq = 1100
    96→let paymentSeq = 4000
    97→
    98→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99→ * 工具
   100→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
   101→function paginate<T>(list: T[], page = 1, pageSize = 20) {
   102→  const total = list.length
   103→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize))
   104→  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize
   105→  return {
   106→    list: list.slice(start, start + pageSize),
   107→    meta: { page, page_size: pageSize, total, total_pages: totalPages },
   108→  }
   109→}
   110→
   111→function nowIso(): string {
   112→  return new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d+Z$/, 'Z')
   113→}
   114→
   115→function categoryChain(categoryId: number): number[] {
   116→  const ids = [categoryId]
   117→  let current = mockCategories.find((item) => item.id === categoryId)
   118→  while (current && current.parent_id) {
   119→    ids.push(current.parent_id)
   120→    current = mockCategories.find((item) => item.id === current?.parent_id)
   121→  }
   122→  return ids
   123→}
   124→
   125→function buildCart(): Cart {
   126→  const items = state.cartItems
   127→  const selected = items.filter((item) => item.selected && item.available)
   128→  const selectedAmount = selected.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price_cents * item.quantity, 0)
   129→  const selectedDiscount = selected.reduce(
   130→    (sum, item) => sum + Math.max(0, item.price_cents - item.price_cents) * item.quantity,
   131→    0,
   132→  )
   133→  return {
   134→    user_id: mockUser.id,
   135→    items,
   136→    total_count: items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0),
   137→    selected_count: selected.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0),
   138→    selected_amount_cents: selectedAmount,
   139→    selected_discount_cents: selectedDiscount,
   140→    selected_payable_cents: selectedAmount - selectedDiscount,
   141→  }
   142→}
   143→
   144→/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→ * 商品域
   146→ * --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
   147→export function listCategories(): ListCategoriesResponse {
   148→  return { categories: mockCategories }
   149→}
   150→
   151→export function listBanners(position?: string): ListBannersResponse {
   152→  const banners: Banner[] = position
   153→    ? mockBanners.filter((banner) => banner.position === position)
   154→    : mockBanners
   155→  return { banners }
   156→}
   157→
   158→export function listProducts(params: {
   159→  page?: number
   160→  page_size?: number
   161→  keyword?: string
   162→  category_id?: number
   163→  min_price_cents?: number
   164→  max_price_cents?: number
   165→  sort?: string
   166→  only_in_stock?: boolean
   167→}): ListProductsResponse {
   168→  let list = mockProducts.filter((product) => product.status === 'PRODUCT_STATUS_ON_SHELF')
   169→
   170→  if (params.category_id) {
   171→    const chain = categoryChain(params.category_id)
   172→    list = list.filter((product) => chain.includes(product.category_id))
   173→  }
   174→  if (params.keyword) {
   175→    const keyword = params.keyword.toLowerCase()
   176→    list = list.filter(
   177→      (product) =>
   178→        product.title.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
   179→        product.subtitle.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
   180→        product.tags.some((tag) => tag.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)),
   181→    )
   182→  }
   183→  if (params.min_price_cents) {
   184→    list = list.filter((product) => product.max_price_cents >= (params.min_price_cents ?? 0))
   185→  }
   186→  if (params.max_price_cents) {
   187→    list = list.filter((product) => product.min_price_cents <= (params.max_price_cents ?? 0))
   188→  }
   189→  if (params.only_in_stock) {
   190→    list = list.filter((product) => product.stock_total > 0)
   191→  }
   192→
   193→  const sorted = [...list]
   194→  switch (params.sort) {
   195→    case 'sales':
   196→      sorted.sort((a, b) => b.sales_count - a.sales_count)
   197→      break
   198→    case 'price_asc':
   199→      sorted.sort((a, b) => a.min_price_cents - b.min_price_cents)
   200→      break
   201→    case 'price_desc':
   202→      sorted.sort((a, b) => b.min_price_cents - a.min_price_cents)
   203→      break
   204→    case 'newest':
   205→      sorted.sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at))
   206→      break
   207→    default:
   208→      sorted.sort((a, b) => b.sales_count - a.sales_count)
   209→  }
   210→
   211→  const page = paginate(sorted, params.page ?? 1, params.page_size ?? 20)
   212→  return { products: page.list, meta: page.meta }
   213→}
   214→
   215→export function productDetail(id: number): ProductDetail {
   216→  const product = mockProducts.find((item) => item.id === id) ?? mockProducts[0]
   217→  const skus = mockSkus.filter((sku) => sku.product_id === product.id)
   218→  const attributes = mockAttributes.filter((item) => item.product_id === product.id)
   219→  const related = mockProducts
   220→    .filter((item) => item.id !== product.id && item.category_id === product.category_id)
   221→    .slice(0, 4)
   222→  const reviews = mockReviews.filter((review) => review.product_id === product.id)
   223→  const good = reviews.filter((review) => review.rating >= 4).length
   224→  const medium = reviews.filter((review) => review.rating === 3).length
   225→  const bad = reviews.filter((review) => review.rating <= 2).length
   226→  const average = reviews.length
   227→    ? reviews.reduce((sum, review) => sum + review.rating, 0) / reviews.length
   228→    : 0
   229→
   230→  return {
   231→    product,
   232→    skus,
   233→    images: [
   234→      { url: product.cover, alt: `${product.title} 主图`, sort: 1 },
   235→      ...skus
   236→        .filter((sku) => sku.image)
   237→        .map((sku, index) => ({ url: sku.image, alt: `${product.title} 细节图 ${index + 1}`, sort: index + 2 })),
   238→    ],
   239→    attributes,
   240→    reviews: [{ total: reviews.length, good, medium, bad, average }],
   241→    review_count: reviews.length,
   242→    review_rating: Number(average.toFixed(2)),
   243→    related,
   244→  }
   245→}
   246→
   247→export function listReviews(params: {
   248→  page?: number
   249→  page_size?: number
   250→  product_id?: number
   251→}): ListReviewsResponse {
   252→  let list = mockReviews
   253→  if (params.product_id) {
   254→    list = list.filter((review) => review.product_id === params.product_id)
   255→  }
   256→  const page = paginate(list, params.page ?? 1, params.page_size ?? 10)
   257→  const good = list.filter((review) => review.rating >= 4).length
   258→  const medium = list.filter((review) => review.rating === 3).length
   259→  const bad = list.filter((review) => review.rating <= 2).length
   260→  const average = list.length ? list.reduce((sum, review) => sum + review.rating, 0) / list.length : 0
   261→  return {
   262→    reviews: page.list,
   263→    meta: page.meta,
   264→    summary: { total: list.length, good, medium, bad, average: Number(average.toFixed(2)) },
   265→  }
   266→}

   269→ * 购物车

   271→export function getCart(): Cart {
   272→  return buildCart()
   273→}
   274→
   275→export function addToCart(req: {
   276→  product_id: number
   277→  sku_id: number
   278→  quantity: number
   279→  selected?: boolean
   280→}): Cart {
   281→  const sku = mockSkus.find((item) => item.id === req.sku_id)
   282→  const product = mockProducts.find((item) => item.id === req.product_id)
   283→  if (!sku || !product) return buildCart()
   284→  const exist = state.cartItems.find(
   285→    (item) => item.product_id === req.product_id && item.sku_id === req.sku_id,
   286→  )
   287→  if (exist) {
   288→    exist.quantity = Math.min(exist.quantity + req.quantity, exist.stock)
   289→    exist.selected = req.selected ?? exist.selected
   290→    exist.available = exist.quantity <= exist.stock
   291→  } else {
   292→    cartSeq += 1
   293→    state.cartItems.push({
   294→      id: cartSeq,
   295→      user_id: mockUser.id,
   296→      product_id: req.product_id,
   297→      sku_id: req.sku_id,
   298→      product_title: product.title,
   299→      spec_text: sku.spec_text,
   300→      image: product.cover,
   301→      price_cents: sku.price_cents,
   302→      quantity: req.quantity,
   303→      stock: sku.stock,
   304→      selected: req.selected ?? true,
   305→      available: req.quantity <= sku.stock,
   306→      subtotal_cents: sku.price_cents * req.quantity,
   307→      created_at: nowIso(),
   308→      updated_at: nowIso(),
   309→    })
   310→  }
   311→  return buildCart()
   312→}
   313→
   314→export function updateCartItem(req: { id: number; quantity?: number; selected?: boolean }): Cart {
   315→  const item = state.cartItems.find((entry) => entry.id === req.id)
   316→  if (item) {
   317→    if (typeof req.quantity === 'number') {
   318→      item.quantity = Math.max(1, req.quantity)
   319→      item.available = item.quantity <= item.stock
   320→      item.subtotal_cents = item.price_cents * item.quantity
   321→    }
   322→    if (typeof req.selected === 'boolean') item.selected = req.selected
   323→    item.updated_at = nowIso()
   324→  }
   325→  return buildCart()
   326→}
   327→
   328→export function removeCartItem(id: number): OperationResult {
   329→  state.cartItems = state.cartItems.filter((item) => item.id !== id)
   330→  return { success: true, message: '已移除商品' }
   331→}
   332→
   333→export function clearCart(): OperationResult {
   334→  state.cartItems = []
   335→  return { success: true, message: '购物车已清空' }
   336→}
   337→
   338→export function setAllSelected(selected: boolean): Cart {
   339→  state.cartItems.forEach((item) => {
   340→    item.selected = selected
   341→  })
   342→  return buildCart()
   343→}
   344→
   345→export function getCartCount(): { count: number } {
   346→  return { count: state.cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0) }
   347→}

   392→export function saveAddress(payload: Partial<Address>): Address {
   393→  if (payload.id) {
   394→    const exist = state.addresses.find((address) => address.id === payload.id)
   395→    if (exist) {
   396→      Object.assign(exist, payload)
   397→      return { ...exist }
   398→    }
   399→  }
   400→  const id = Math.max(0, ...state.addresses.map((address) => address.id)) + 1
   401→  const address: Address = {
   402→    id,
   403→    receiver: payload.receiver ?? '',
   404→    phone: payload.phone ?? '',
   405→    province: payload.province ?? '',
   406→    city: payload.city ?? '',
   407→    district: payload.district ?? '',
   408→    detail: payload.detail ?? '',
   409→    postal_code: payload.postal_code ?? '',
   410→    tag: payload.tag ?? '家',
   411→    is_default: payload.is_default ?? state.addresses.length === 0,
   412→  }
   413→  if (address.is_default) {
   414→    state.addresses.forEach((item) => {
   415→      item.is_default = false
   416→    })
   417→  }
   418→  state.addresses.push(address)
   419→  return { ...address }
   420→}

   439→export function toggleFavorite(productId: number): { favorited: boolean } {
   440→  const index = state.favorites.findIndex((item) => item.product_id === productId)
   441→  if (index >= 0) {
   442→    state.favorites.splice(index, 1)
   443→    return { favorited: false }
   444→  }
   445→  const product = mockProducts.find((item) => item.id === productId)
   446→  if (!product) return { favorited: false }
   447→  state.favorites.unshift({
   448→    id: Date.now(),
   449→    product_id: product.id,
   450→    title: product.title,
   451→    cover: product.cover,
   452→    price_cents: product.min_price_cents,
   453→    created_at: nowIso(),
   454→  })
   455→  return { favorited: true }
   456→}

import { fileURLToPath, URL } from 'node:url'

import vue from '@vitejs/plugin-vue'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

// 开发环境把 /api 代理到后端网关，避免浏览器跨域限制。
// 需要指向其它网关时设置 VITE_GATEWAY_URL 环境变量。

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [vue()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': fileURLToPath(new URL('./src', import.meta.url)),
    },
  },
  server: {
    port: 5173,
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: gateway,
        changeOrigin: true,
      },
    },
  },
  preview: {
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: gateway,
        changeOrigin: true,
      },
    },
  },
})

   713→      timeStamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)),

   724→  state.payments.unshift(payment)
   725→  return { ...payment }
   726→}

   731→    state.payments.find((item) => item.order_id === params.order_id) ??
   732→    createPayment({ order_id: params.order_id ?? 0, channel: 1 })
   733→  return { ...payment }
   734→}

   736→/**
   737→ * 模拟支付回调：把支付单置为成功，并把订单推进到「待发货」
   738→ */
   739→export function notifyPayment(payload: {
   740→  payment_no?: string
   741→  status?: number
   742→  transaction_id?: string
   743→}): OperationResult {
   744→  const payment = payload.payment_no
   745→    ? state.payments.find((item) => item.payment_no === payload.payment_no)
   746→    : state.payments[0]
   747→  if (!payment) return { success: false, message: '支付单不存在' }
   748→  payment.status = payload.status === 3 ? 'PAYMENT_STATUS_FAILED' : 'PAYMENT_STATUS_SUCCESS'
   749→  payment.transaction_id = payload.transaction_id || `txn${Date.now()}`
   750→  payment.paid_at = nowIso()
   751→  payment.updated_at = nowIso()
   752→  if (payment.status === 'PAYMENT_STATUS_SUCCESS') {
   753→    const order = state.orders.find((item) => item.id === payment.order_id)
   754→    if (order && order.status === 'ORDER_STATUS_PENDING_PAYMENT') {

   756→      order.paid_cents = order.payable_cents
   757→      order.paid_at = nowIso()
   758→      order.updated_at = nowIso()
   759→    }
   760→  }
   761→  return { success: true, message: '支付回调处理成功' }
   762→}

   769→  if (params.status) {
   770→    const target = `USER_COUPON_STATUS_${['UNUSED', 'USED', 'EXPIRED'][params.status - 1]}`
   771→    list = list.filter((item) => item.status === target)
   772→  }
   773→  const page = paginate(list, params.page ?? 1, 20)
   774→  return {

   781→
   782→export function previewCheckout(req: {
   783→  items?: { sku_id: number; product_id: number; quantity: number; price_cents: number }[]
   784→  coupon_id?: number
   785→  points_used?: number
   786→}): PreviewCheckoutResponse {
   787→  const items = req.items ?? []
   788→  const totalAmount = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price_cents * item.quantity, 0)
   789→  const coupon = state.userCoupons.find(
   790→    (item) => item.coupon_id === req.coupon_id && item.status === 'USER_COUPON_STATUS_UNUSED',
   791→  )
   792→  let couponDiscount = 0
   793→  if (coupon) {
   794→    if (coupon.coupon.type === 'COUPON_TYPE_FIXED') {
   795→      couponDiscount = Math.min(coupon.coupon.face_value_cents, totalAmount)
   796→    } else if (coupon.coupon.type === 'COUPON_TYPE_PERCENT') {
   797→      couponDiscount = Math.min(
   798→        Math.round((totalAmount * (100 - coupon.coupon.percent)) / 100),
   799→        coupon.coupon.max_discount_cents,
   800→      )
   801→    } else {
   802→      couponDiscount = 0
   803→    }
   804→  }
   805→  const pointsDiscount = Math.min((req.points_used ?? 0) * 100, mockUser.points * 100, totalAmount)
   806→  const freight = totalAmount >= 9900 ? 0 : 800
   807→  const available = state.userCoupons.filter(
   808→    (item) =>
   809→      item.status === 'USER_COUPON_STATUS_UNUSED' &&
   810→      item.coupon.min_amount_cents <= totalAmount,
   811→  )
   812→  return {
   813→    total_amount_cents: totalAmount,
   814→    discount_cents: couponDiscount + pointsDiscount,
   815→    coupon_discount_cents: couponDiscount,
   816→    points_discount_cents: pointsDiscount,
   817→    freight_cents: freight,
   818→    payable_cents: Math.max(0, totalAmount - couponDiscount - pointsDiscount + freight),
   819→    available_coupons: available,
   820→    active_promotions: mockPromotions,
   821→  }
   822→}

########## src/api/payment.ts
import { httpGet, httpPost } from '@/api/client'
import * as mock from '@/mock'
import type { OperationResult, Payment } from '@/types'

/** 支付渠道 */
export const PAYMENT_CHANNELS = [
  { value: 1, key: 'wechat', label: '微信支付', desc: '推荐使用，秒级到账', icon: '💚' },
  { value: 2, key: 'alipay', label: '支付宝', desc: '支持花呗分期', icon: '💙' },
  { value: 3, key: 'balance', label: '余额支付', desc: '使用账户余额支付', icon: '🧡' },
] as const

/** 创建支付单 */
export function createPayment(payload: {
  order_id: number
  channel: number
  return_url?: string
}): Promise<Payment> {
  return httpPost<Payment>(
    '/api/v1/payments',
    payload,
    { fallback: () => mock.createPayment(payload) },
  )
}

/** 查询支付结果 */
export function queryPayment(params: {
  payment_id?: number
  order_id?: number
}): Promise<Payment> {
  return httpGet<Payment>(
    '/api/v1/payments/query',
    { payment_id: params.payment_id, order_id: params.order_id },
    { fallback: () => mock.queryPayment(params) },
  )
}

/** 支付结果回调（演示环境用于模拟第三方支付完成） */
export function notifyPayment(payload: {
  payment_no: string
  transaction_id?: string
  signature?: string
  raw_body?: string
  status?: number
}): Promise<OperationResult> {
  return httpPost<OperationResult>(
    '/api/v1/payments/notify',
    payload,
    { fallback: () => mock.notifyPayment(payload) },
  )
}
########## src/api/logistics.ts
import { httpGet } from '@/api/client'
import * as mock from '@/mock'
import type { ListShipmentTracksResponse } from '@/types'

/** 查询订单物流（返回运单与轨迹） */
export function getShipment(orderId: number): Promise<ListShipmentTracksResponse> {
  return httpGet<ListShipmentTracksResponse>(
    `/api/v1/logistics/order/${orderId}`,
    undefined,
    { fallback: () => mock.shipmentTracks(orderId) },
  )
}

/** 按订单号查询物流轨迹 */
export function listTracks(orderId: number): Promise<ListShipmentTracksResponse> {
  return httpGet<ListShipmentTracksResponse>(
    '/api/v1/logistics/tracks',
    { order_id: orderId },
    { fallback: () => mock.shipmentTracks(orderId) },
  )
}
########## src/api/notification.ts
import { httpGet, httpPost, httpPut } from '@/api/client'
import * as mock from '@/mock'
import type { ListNotificationsResponse, OperationResult } from '@/types'

/** 通知列表 */
export function listNotifications(params: {
  page?: number
  page_size?: number
  type?: number
  unread_only?: boolean
}): Promise<ListNotificationsResponse> {
  return httpGet<ListNotificationsResponse>(
    '/api/v1/notifications',
    {
      'page.page': params.page,
      'page.page_size': params.page_size,
      type: params.type,
      unread_only: params.unread_only,
    },
    { fallback: () => mock.listNotifications(params) },
  )
}

/** 标记已读 */
export function markRead(id: number): Promise<OperationResult> {
  return httpPut<OperationResult>(
    `/api/v1/notifications/${id}/read`,
    {},
    { fallback: () => mock.markNotificationRead(id) },
  )
}

/** 全部标记已读 */
export function markAllRead(): Promise<OperationResult> {
  return httpPost<OperationResult>(
    '/api/v1/notifications/read-all',
    {},
    { fallback: () => mock.markAllNotificationsRead() },
  )
}

/** 未读数量 */
export function getUnreadCount(): Promise<{ count: number }> {
  return httpGet<{ count: number }>(
    '/api/v1/notifications/unread-count',
    undefined,
    { fallback: () => mock.unreadCount() },
  )
}

########## src/components/Pagination.vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'

/** 分页器 */
const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<{
    page: number
    pageSize?: number
    total: number
  }>(),
  {
    pageSize: 20,
    total: 0,
  },
)

const emit = defineEmits<{
  change: [page: number]
}>()

const totalPages = computed(() => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(props.total / props.pageSize)))

/** 页码窗口：当前页前后各 2 页 */
const pages = computed<(number | '...')[]>(() => {
  const last = totalPages.value
  const current = props.page
  if (last <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: last }, (_, index) => index + 1)
  }
  const result: (number | '...')[] = [1]
  const start = Math.max(2, current - 2)
  const end = Math.min(last - 1, current + 2)
  if (start > 2) result.push('...')
  for (let i = start; i <= end; i += 1) result.push(i)
  if (end < last - 1) result.push('...')
  result.push(last)
  return result
})

function go(page: number): void {
  if (page < 1 || page > totalPages.value || page === props.page) return
  emit('change', page)
}
</script>

<template>
  <nav v-if="totalPages > 1" class="pagination">
    <button
      type="button"
      class="pagination__btn"
      :disabled="props.page <= 1"
      @click="go(props.page - 1)"
    >
      上一页
    </button>
    <button
      v-for="(item, index) in pages"
      :key="`${item}-${index}`"
      type="button"
      class="pagination__btn"
      :class="{ 'pagination__btn--active': item === props.page }"
      :disabled="item === '...'"
      @click="typeof item === 'number' && go(item)"
    >
      {{ item }}
    </button>
    <button
      type="button"
      class="pagination__btn"
      :disabled="props.page >= totalPages"
      @click="go(props.page + 1)"
    >
      下一页
    </button>
    <span class="pagination__total">共 {{ props.total }} 条 / {{ totalPages }} 页</span>
  </nav>
</template>

<style scoped>
.pagination {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--ss-space-2);
  padding: var(--ss-space-5) 0;
}

  cursor: not-allowed;
}

  font-size: var(--ss-font-size-sm);

<script setup lang="ts">

import { formatYuan } from '@/utils/format'

/** 金额展示：现价 + 划线原价 */
const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<{
    cents?: number | null
    originalCents?: number | null
    size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'
    tone?: 'brand' | 'danger' | 'default'
  }>(),
  {
    cents: 0,
    originalCents: 0,
    size: 'md',
    tone: 'danger',
  },
)

const hasOriginal = computed(() => (props.originalCents ?? 0) > (props.cents ?? 0))
</script>

<template>
  <span class="price-text">
    <span class="price-text__now" :class="`price-text__now--${props.size}`">
      {{ formatYuan(props.cents) }}
    </span>
    <del v-if="hasOriginal" class="price-text__origin">
      {{ formatYuan(props.originalCents) }}
    </del>
  </span>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

import PriceText from '@/components/PriceText.vue'
import type { Product } from '@/types'
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/format'
import { handleImageError } from '@/utils/image'

/** 商品卡片 */
const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<{
    product: Product
    showAdd?: boolean
    loading?: boolean
  }>(),
  {
    showAdd: true,
    loading: false,
  },
)

const emit = defineEmits<{
  add: [product: Product]
}>()

const router = useRouter()

const hasDiscount = computed(() => props.product.max_price_cents > props.product.min_price_cents)
const soldOut = computed(() => props.product.stock_total <= 0)

function openDetail(): void {
  router.push({ name: 'product', params: { id: props.product.id } })
}
</script>

<template>
  <article class="product-card ss-card ss-card-hover">
    <div class="product-card__media" @click="openDetail">
      <img
        :src="props.product.cover"
        :alt="props.product.title"
        loading="lazy"
        @error="handleImageError"
      />
      <span v-if="soldOut" class="product-card__soldout">已售罄</span>
      <span v-else-if="hasDiscount" class="product-card__discount">
        省 {{ formatCount(props.product.max_price_cents - props.product.min_price_cents) }} 分
      </span>
    </div>

    <div class="product-card__body">
      <h3 class="product-card__title ss-ellipsis" :title="props.product.title" @click="openDetail">
        {{ props.product.title }}
      </h3>
      <p class="product-card__subtitle ss-ellipsis">{{ props.product.subtitle }}</p>

      <div class="product-card__tags">
        <span v-for="tag in props.product.tags.slice(0, 2)" :key="tag" class="ss-tag ss-tag-neutral">
          {{ tag }}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div class="product-card__footer">
        <div class="ss-col">
          <PriceText :cents="props.product.min_price_cents" size="md" />
          <span class="ss-muted-block">已售 {{ formatCount(props.product.sales_count) }}</span>
        </div>
        <button
          v-if="props.showAdd"
          type="button"
          class="ss-btn ss-btn-primary ss-btn-sm"
          :disabled="soldOut || props.loading"
          @click.stop="emit('add', props.product)"
        >
          加入购物车
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </article>
</template>

<style scoped>

  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  height: 100%;
}

}

// EXTRA_MARKER: 由 scripts/extend-proto.py 追加的消息定义

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 频道 / 楼层
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message Channel {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string code = 3;
  string icon = 4;
  string banner_url = 5;
  string description = 6;
  int32 sort = 7;
  int32 type = 8;   // 1 推荐 2 新品 3 热销 4 限时 5 预售 6 清仓 7 包邮 8 跨境 9 楼层
  int32 status = 9; // 0 停用 1 启用
  int32 product_count = 10;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 11;
}

message ListChannelsRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
  string keyword = 2;
  int32 type = 3;
}

message ListChannelsResponse {
  repeated Channel channels = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message GetChannelRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

message ListChannelProductsRequest {
  int64 channel_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
  string keyword = 3;
  string sort = 4; // default | sales | price_asc | price_desc | newest
}

message ListChannelProductsResponse {
  Channel channel = 1;
  repeated Product products = 2;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 3;
}

message SaveChannelRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string code = 3;
  string icon = 4;
  string banner_url = 5;
  string description = 6;
  int32 sort = 7;
  int32 type = 8;
  int32 status = 9;
}

message DeleteChannelRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 评价标签
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message ReviewTag {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  int32 sort = 3;
  bool enabled = 4;
}

message ListReviewTagsRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
  string keyword = 2;
}

message ListReviewTagsResponse {
  repeated ReviewTag tags = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message SaveReviewTagRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  int32 sort = 3;
  bool enabled = 4;
}

message DeleteReviewTagRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 商品保障项
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message Guarantee {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string icon = 3;
  string description = 4;
  int32 sort = 5;
  bool enabled = 6;
}

message ListGuaranteesRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
}

message ListGuaranteesResponse {
  repeated Guarantee guarantees = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message SaveGuaranteeRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string icon = 3;
  string description = 4;
  int32 sort = 5;
  bool enabled = 6;
}

message DeleteGuaranteeRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 商品参数
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message ListProductParamsRequest {
  int64 product_id = 1;
}

message ListProductParamsResponse {
  repeated ProductAttribute params = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 商品问答
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message ProductQuestion {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 product_id = 2;
  string product_title = 3;
  int64 user_id = 4;
  string user_nickname = 5;
  string content = 6;
  repeated string images = 7;
  string reply = 8;
  string reply_at = 9;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 10;
}

message ListProductQuestionsRequest {
  int64 product_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
}

message ListProductQuestionsResponse {
  repeated ProductQuestion questions = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message CreateQuestionRequest {
  int64 product_id = 1;
  string content = 2;
  repeated string images = 3;
}

message AnswerQuestionRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string reply = 2;
}

message ListQuestionsRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
  string keyword = 2;
  int64 product_id = 3;
}

message ListQuestionsResponse {
  repeated ProductQuestion questions = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}
```

}
}

// EXTRA_MARKER: 由 scripts/extend-proto.py 追加的消息定义

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 签到
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message SignInRequest {}

message SignInCalendarDay {
  string date = 1;
  bool signed = 2;
  int32 points = 3;
}

message GetSignInStatusRequest {}

message GetSignInStatusResponse {
  bool signed_today = 1;
  int32 continuous_days = 2;
  int32 total_days = 3;
  int32 points_awarded = 4;
  int32 next_points = 5;
  repeated SignInCalendarDay calendar = 6;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 任务中心
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message MarketingTask {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string description = 3;
  int32 type = 4;   // 1 浏览 2 下单 3 分享 4 评价 5 签到 6 邀请
  int32 target_count = 5;
  int32 reward_points = 6;
  int64 reward_coupon_id = 7;
  int32 sort = 8;
  bool enabled = 9;
  string icon = 10;
}

message UserTask {
  int64 task_id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string description = 3;
  int32 type = 4;
  int32 progress = 5;
  int32 target_count = 6;
  bool completed = 7;
  bool claimed = 8;
  int32 reward_points = 9;
  int64 reward_coupon_id = 10;
  string icon = 11;
}

message ListTasksRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
}

message ListTasksResponse {
  repeated UserTask tasks = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message ClaimTaskRequest {
  int64 task_id = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 活动详情 / 场次
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// PromotionProduct 是营销域内嵌的商品摘要，避免跨域依赖商品域消息。
message PromotionProduct {
  int64 id = 1;
  string title = 2;
  string image = 3;
  int64 min_price_cents = 4;
  int64 max_price_cents = 5;
  int32 stock_total = 6;
  int32 sales = 7;
  int32 status = 8;
}

message GetPromotionRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

message GetPromotionResponse {
  Promotion promotion = 1;
  repeated PromotionProduct products = 2;
  repeated PromotionSession sessions = 3;
}

message ListPromotionProductsRequest {
  int64 promotion_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
}

message ListPromotionProductsResponse {
  repeated PromotionProduct products = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message PromotionSession {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 promotion_id = 2;
  string name = 3;
  int64 start_time = 4; // unix 秒
  int64 end_time = 5;   // unix 秒
  int32 stock = 6;
  int32 sold_count = 7;
  int32 status = 8; // 0 未开始 1 进行中 2 已结束
}

message ListPromotionSessionsRequest {
  int64 promotion_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
}

message ListPromotionSessionsResponse {
  repeated PromotionSession sessions = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message SavePromotionSessionRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 promotion_id = 2;
  string name = 3;
  int64 start_time = 4;
  int64 end_time = 5;
  int32 stock = 6;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 拼团
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message GroupBuy {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string description = 3;
  int64 promotion_id = 4;
  int64 product_id = 5;
  string product_title = 6;
  string product_image = 7;
  int64 price_cents = 8;
  int64 original_price_cents = 9;
  int32 required_members = 10;
  int32 joined_members = 11;
  int32 group_size = 12;
  int32 status = 13; // 0 未开始 1 进行中 2 已结束 3 已下线
  int64 start_time = 14;
  int64 end_time = 15;
  string banner_image = 16;
}

message GroupBuyMember {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 group_buy_id = 2;
  int64 user_id = 3;
  string user_nickname = 4;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp joined_at = 5;
}

message ListGroupBuysRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
  string keyword = 2;
  int32 status = 3;
}

message ListGroupBuysResponse {
  repeated GroupBuy group_buys = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message GetGroupBuyRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

message GetGroupBuyResponse {
  GroupBuy group_buy = 1;
  repeated GroupBuyMember members = 2;
}

message JoinGroupBuyRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  int32 quantity = 2;
}

message SaveGroupBuyRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string description = 3;
  int64 promotion_id = 4;
  int64 product_id = 5;
  int64 price_cents = 6;
  int32 required_members = 7;
  int64 start_time = 8;
  int64 end_time = 9;
  string banner_image = 10;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 会员等级说明
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message MarketingUserLevel {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  int32 level = 3;
  int32 min_growth = 4;
  int32 max_growth = 5;
  int32 discount_percent = 6;
  string icon = 7;
  string description = 8;
  repeated string benefits = 9;
  int32 sort = 10;
}

message ListUserLevelsRequest {}

message ListUserLevelsResponse {
  repeated MarketingUserLevel levels = 1;
}
```

</details>

// EXTRA_MARKER: 由 scripts/extend-proto.py 追加的消息定义

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 会员等级与成长值
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message UserLevel {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  int32 level = 3;
  int32 min_growth = 4;
  int32 max_growth = 5;
  int32 discount_percent = 6; // 折扣百分比，100 表示无折扣
  string icon = 7;
  string description = 8;
  repeated string benefits = 9;
  int32 sort = 10;
}

message GetUserLevelRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
}

message UserLevelInfo {
  int32 level = 1;
  string level_name = 2;
  int32 growth = 3;
  int32 current_min_growth = 4;
  int32 next_min_growth = 5;
  int32 growth_to_next = 6;
  int32 discount_percent = 7;
  string next_level_name = 8;
  repeated UserLevel levels = 9;
}

message ListUserLevelsRequest {}

message ListUserLevelsResponse {
  repeated UserLevel levels = 1;
}

message ListGrowthLogsRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
}

message GrowthLog {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 user_id = 2;
  int32 change = 3;
  int32 balance = 4;
  string title = 5;
  string biz_type = 6;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 7;
}

message ListGrowthLogsResponse {
  repeated GrowthLog logs = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
  int32 current_growth = 3;
}

message SaveUserLevelRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  int32 level = 3;
  int32 min_growth = 4;
  int32 max_growth = 5;
  int32 discount_percent = 6;
  string icon = 7;
  string description = 8;
  repeated string benefits = 9;
  int32 sort = 10;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 行政区域
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message Region {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 parent_id = 2;
  string name = 3;
  string code = 4;
  int32 level = 5; // 1 省 2 市 3 区
  int32 sort = 6;
}

message ListRegionsRequest {
  int64 parent_id = 1;
  bool tree = 2;
}

message ListRegionsResponse {
  repeated Region regions = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 发票抬头模板
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message InvoiceTemplate {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 user_id = 2;
  string title = 3;
  string tax_no = 4;
  string type = 5; // personal | company
  string email = 6;
  string phone = 7;
  string address = 8;
  string bank_name = 9;
  string bank_account = 10;
  bool is_default = 11;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 12;
}

message ListInvoiceTemplatesRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
}

######### src/views/FlashSaleView.vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

import EmptyState from '@/components/EmptyState.vue'
import PriceText from '@/components/PriceText.vue'
import { listPromotions } from '@/api/marketing'
import { getProduct, listProducts } from '@/api/product'
import { useAppStore } from '@/stores/app'
import type { Product, Promotion } from '@/types'
import { formatCountdown, formatNumber } from '@/utils/format'
import { handleImageError } from '@/utils/image'

const router = useRouter()
const app = useAppStore()

const promotions = ref<Promotion[]>([])
const products = ref<Product[]>([])
const loading = ref(false)
const buying = ref<number | null>(null)
const now = ref(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000))
let timer: number | null = null

/** 秒杀活动：promotion.type === 3 */
const flashPromotions = computed(() => promotions.value.filter((item) => item.type === 3))

/** 距离最近一个活动结束的倒计时 */
const nearestEnd = computed(() => {
  const ends = flashPromotions.value.map((item) => item.end_time).filter((value) => value > now.value)
  if (!ends.length) return null
  const end = Math.min(...ends)
  return { end, remain: end - now.value, text: formatCountdown(end - now.value) }
})

/** 活动对应的商品 */
function productOf(promotion: Promotion): Product | undefined {
  if (promotion.product_id) {
    return products.value.find((item) => item.id === promotion.product_id)
  }
  return products.value[0]
}

/** 已抢进度 */
function soldPercent(promotion: Promotion): number {
  const limit = promotion.limit_per_user || 0
  if (!limit) return 0
  return Math.min(100, Math.round((promotion.sold_count / limit) * 100))
}

async function grab(promotion: Promotion): Promise<void> {
  const product = productOf(promotion)
  if (!product) {
    app.warning('活动商品暂不可售')
    return
  }
  buying.value = promotion.id
  try {
    const detail = await getProduct(product.id)
    const sku = detail.skus.find((item) => item.enabled && item.stock > 0)
    if (!sku) {
      app.warning('该商品暂时缺货')
      return
    }
    router.push({
      name: 'checkout',
      query: {
        productId: String(product.id),
        skuId: String(sku.id),
        quantity: '1',
      },
    })
  } catch {
    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
  } finally {
    buying.value = null
  }
}

async function load(): Promise<void> {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const [promotionRes, productRes] = await Promise.all([
      listPromotions({ status: 1 }),
      listProducts({ page: 1, page_size: 12, sort: 'sales' }),
    ])
    promotions.value = promotionRes.promotions
    products.value = productRes.products
  } catch {
    // 错误提示已由接口层处理
  } finally {

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

import EmptyState from '@/components/EmptyState.vue'
import PriceText from '@/components/PriceText.vue'
import { listPromotions } from '@/api/marketing'
import { getProduct, listProducts } from '@/api/product'
import { useAppStore } from '@/stores/app'
import type { Product, Promotion } from '@/types'
import { formatCountdown, formatNumber } from '@/utils/format'
import { handleImageError } from '@/utils/image'

const router = useRouter()
const app = useAppStore()

const promotions = ref<Promotion[]>([])
const products = ref<Product[]>([])
const loading = ref(false)
const joining = ref(false)
const now = ref(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000))
const activePromotion = ref<Promotion | null>(null)
const activeProduct = ref<Product | null>(null)
const groupSize = ref(3)
let timer: number | null = null

/** 拼团活动：promotion.type === 4 */
const groupPromotions = computed(() => promotions.value.filter((item) => item.type === 4))

/** 其它进行中的活动（后端暂无拼团种子数据时用于兜底展示） */
const otherPromotions = computed(() => promotions.value.filter((item) => item.type !== 4))

const nearestEnd = computed(() => {
  const ends = groupPromotions.value.map((item) => item.end_time).filter((value) => value > now.value)
  if (!ends.length) return null
  const end = Math.min(...ends)
  return { end, remain: end - now.value, text: formatCountdown(end - now.value) }
})

function productOf(promotion: Promotion): Product | undefined {
  if (promotion.product_id) {
    return products.value.find((item) => item.id === promotion.product_id)
  }
  return products.value[0]
}

/** 成团进度（已参团人数 / 目标人数） */
function joinedCount(promotion: Promotion): number {
  return promotion.sold_count || 0
}

function targetCount(promotion: Promotion): number {
  return promotion.limit_per_user || Math.max(3, joinedCount(promotion))
}

function groupPercent(promotion: Promotion): number {
  const target = targetCount(promotion)
  if (!target) return 0
  return Math.min(100, Math.round((joinedCount(promotion) / target) * 100))
}

function openGroup(promotion: Promotion): void {
  activePromotion.value = promotion
  activeProduct.value = productOf(promotion) ?? null
  groupSize.value = targetCount(promotion)
}

function closeGroup(): void {
  activePromotion.value = null
  activeProduct.value = null
}

async function joinGroup(): Promise<void> {
  const promotion = activePromotion.value
  const product = activeProduct.value
  if (!promotion || !product) {
    app.warning('拼团商品不可用')
    return
  }
  joining.value = true
  try {
    const detail = await getProduct(product.id)
    const sku = detail.skus.find((item) => item.enabled && item.stock > 0)
    if (!sku) {
      app.warning('该商品暂时缺货')
      return
    }
    closeGroup()

      name: 'checkout',

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'

import { listPointsLogs, getProfile } from '@/api/user'
import { listUserCoupons } from '@/api/marketing'
import { getOrderCount } from '@/api/order'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { useUserStore } from '@/stores/user'
import type { OrderCount, User } from '@/types'
import { formatNumber, formatYuan } from '@/utils/format'
import { handleImageError } from '@/utils/image'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const userStore = useUserStore()

const profile = ref<User | null>(null)
const totalEarned = ref(0)
const couponCount = ref(0)
const orderCount = ref<OrderCount>({
  pending_payment: 0,
  pending_shipment: 0,
  pending_receipt: 0,
  refunding: 0,
})

/** 会员等级：proto 中 User 没有 level 字段，这里按积分换算等级 */
const LEVELS = [
  { name: '青铜会员', min: 0, next: 1000, icon: '🥉' },
  { name: '白银会员', min: 1000, next: 3000, icon: '🥈' },
  { name: '黄金会员', min: 3000, next: 6000, icon: '🥇' },
  { name: '钻石会员', min: 6000, next: 12000, icon: '💎' },
]

const level = computed(() => {
  const points = profile.value?.points ?? 0
  let current = LEVELS[0]
  for (const item of LEVELS) {
    if (points >= item.min) current = item
  }
  const next = LEVELS.find((item) => item.min > current.min) ?? null
  const progress = next ? Math.min(100, Math.round(((points - current.min) / (next.min - current.min)) * 100)) : 100
  return { current, next, progress }
})

const benefits = computed(() => [
  { label: '积分中心', icon: '🎯', desc: '积分明细与兑换', path: '/points' },
  { label: '我的优惠券', icon: '🎟️', desc: `已获得 ${couponCount.value} 张`, path: '/user/coupons' },
  { label: '领券中心', icon: '🧧', desc: '领取新优惠券', path: '/coupon-center' },
  { label: '发票管理', icon: '🧾', desc: '申请与下载发票', path: '/invoices' },
  { label: '物流跟踪', icon: '🚚', desc: '查询包裹轨迹', path: '/logistics' },
  { label: '订单轨迹', icon: '🧭', desc: '订单状态流转记录', path: '/order-trace' },
  { label: '售后申请', icon: '↩️', desc: '订单退款处理', path: '/refund' },
  { label: '我的评价', icon: '⭐', desc: '发表与查看评价', path: '/reviews' },
  { label: '浏览足迹', icon: '🕘', desc: '最近浏览的商品', path: '/history' },
  { label: '商品对比', icon: '⚖️', desc: '横向对比商品参数', path: '/compare' },
])

async function load(): Promise<void> {
  const [profileRes, pointsRes, couponRes, countRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    getProfile(),
    listPointsLogs({ page: 1, page_size: 100 }),
    listUserCoupons({ page: 1, page_size: 50 }),
    getOrderCount(),
  ])
  if (profileRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    profile.value = profileRes.value
    auth.user = profileRes.value
  }
  if (pointsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    totalEarned.value = pointsRes.value.logs
      .filter((log) => log.change > 0)
      .reduce((sum, log) => sum + log.change, 0)
  }
  if (couponRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    couponCount.value = couponRes.value.coupons.length
  }
  if (countRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    orderCount.value = countRes.value
  }
  await userStore.loadProfile().catch(() => undefined)
}

onMounted(load)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="member ss-container ss-page">
    <section class="member__hero">
      <img
        class="member__avatar"
```
