import { ref } from 'vue'

import { getProduct } from '@/api/product'
import { useAppStore } from '@/stores/app'
import { useCartStore } from '@/stores/cart'

/**
 * 加入购物车
 * 商品卡片只有商品信息，需要先取详情拿到默认 SKU。
 */
export function useAddToCart() {
  const cart = useCartStore()
  const app = useAppStore()
  const loading = ref(false)

  async function add(productId: number, quantity = 1): Promise<boolean> {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const detail = await getProduct(productId)
      const sku = detail.skus.find((item) => item.enabled && item.stock > 0)
      if (!sku) {
        app.error('该商品暂时缺货')
        return false
      }
      await cart.addToCart({
        product_id: productId,
        sku_id: sku.id,
        quantity,
      })
      app.success('已加入购物车')
      return true
    } catch {
      // 失败提示由接口层统一处理
      return false
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  return { add, loading }
}
=== app store ===
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref } from 'vue'

export type ToastType = 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error'

export interface ToastItem {
  id: number
  type: ToastType
  message: string
}

/**
 * 全局提示与演示模式状态。
 * 后端未就绪时接口层会切到内置 Mock 数据，这里统一提示用户。
 */
export const useAppStore = defineStore('app', () => {
  const toasts = ref<ToastItem[]>([])
  const demoMode = ref(false)
  const demoReason = ref('')
  let seq = 0

  function toast(message: string, type: ToastType = 'info', duration = 2800): void {
    if (!message) return
    seq += 1
    const id = seq
    toasts.value.push({ id, type, message })
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      toasts.value = toasts.value.filter((item) => item.id !== id)
    }, duration)
  }

  function success(message: string): void {
    toast(message, 'success')
  }

  function warning(message: string): void {
    toast(message, 'warning')
  }

  function info(message: string): void {
    toast(message, 'info')
  }

  function error(message: string): void {
    toast(message, 'error')
  }

  /** 进入演示数据模式 */
  function enterDemoMode(reason: string): void {
    if (!demoMode.value) {
      demoMode.value = true
      demoReason.value = reason
    }
  }

  /** 恢复真实数据模式 */
  function exitDemoMode(): void {
    demoMode.value = false
    demoReason.value = ''
  }

  function dismiss(id: number): void {
    toasts.value = toasts.value.filter((item) => item.id !== id)
  }

  return { toasts, demoMode, demoReason, toast, success, warning, info, error, enterDemoMode, exitDemoMode, dismiss }
})
=== image util ===
/** 图片占位与加载失败处理 */

export const FALLBACK_IMAGE =
  "data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='400' height='400'%3E%3Crect width='400' height='400' fill='%23EEF4FF'/%3E%3Ctext x='50%25' y='50%25' font-family='sans-serif' font-size='26' fill='%232A4AD9' text-anchor='middle' dominant-baseline='middle'%3EShopStack%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E"

/** 图片加载失败时替换为占位图（只替换一次，避免死循环） */
export function handleImageError(event: Event): void {
  const target = event.target as HTMLImageElement | null
  if (!target) return
  if (target.dataset.fallbackApplied === '1') return
  target.dataset.fallbackApplied = '1'
  target.src = FALLBACK_IMAGE
}
```

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'

/** 星级评分 */
const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<{
    rating?: number
    size?: 'sm' | 'md'
  }>(),
  {
    rating: 0,
    size: 'md',
  },
)

const stars = computed(() => {
  const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Number(props.rating ?? 0)))
  return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((index) => (value >= index - 0.25 ? 'full' : value >= index - 0.75 ? 'half' : 'empty'))
})
</script>

<template>
  <span class="star-rating" :class="`star-rating--${props.size}`">
    <span
      v-for="(state, index) in stars"
      :key="index"
      class="star-rating__star"
      :class="`star-rating__star--${state}`"
      >★</span
    >
    <span class="star-rating__value">{{ Number(props.rating ?? 0).toFixed(1) }}</span>
  </span>
</template>

<style scoped>
.star-rating {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2px;
  color: var(--ss-color-warning-500);
}

.star-rating--sm {
  font-size: var(--ss-font-size-xs);
}

.star-rating--md {
  font-size: var(--ss-font-size-base);
}

.star-rating__star--empty {
  color: var(--ss-color-neutral-300);
}

.star-rating__star--half {
  opacity: 0.6;
}

.star-rating__value {
  margin-left: var(--ss-space-1);
  color: var(--ss-color-text-tertiary);
  font-family: var(--ss-font-family-numeric);
  font-size: var(--ss-font-size-sm);
}
</style>
```

  cancel: '取消订单',
  refund: '申请退款',
  close: '订单关闭',
  review: '发表评价',
}

const hasLogs = computed(() => logs.value.length > 0)

async function query(): Promise<void> {
  const value = Number(orderInput.value)
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
    app.warning('请输入正确的订单 ID')
    return
  }
  loading.value = true
  searched.value = true
  try {
    const result = await listOrderLogs(value)
    logs.value = result.logs ?? []
    if (!logs.value.length) {
      app.info('该订单暂无状态流转记录')
    }
  } catch {
    app.error('订单轨迹查询失败，请稍后重试')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

    orders.value = result.orders
